### [1] NSE:LTIM — Weekly POC Reversion (SHORT)
* **Trigger Details:** Entry: 4055.40 | Stop Loss: 4110.00 | Exit: 4110.00 | Loss: -54.60
* **Mistake Diagnosis:** Outside-Value Open Reversion Trap. Weekly/Monthly POC Reversion was triggered, but the contract opened outside the previous cycle's Value Area (equilibrium range).
* **Auto-Learned Parameter / Constraint Adjustment:** `POC Reversion Constraint: Weekly/Monthly POC reversions are ONLY valid if the contract opens strictly INSIDE the previous cycle's value area.`
